1	# Notes
2	
3	This directory contains various notes and documentation related to the project.
4	Each file is organized by topic for easy reference.
5	
6	By default there are chores-*.md and todo.md. Chores are general notes
7	about tasks and todo.md contains short term tasks and their status.
8	
9	App architecture — module map, the CLI-args / `Context`+`Params`
10	split, the subcommand model, and the in-flight migrations — lives
11	at the repo root in [`../ARCHITECTURE.md`](../ARCHITECTURE.md).
12	
13	In the future we I expect we may want to create a "notes"
14	database to better manage the information, TBD.
15	
16	Examples chore file:
17	```
18	# Chores-01.md
19	 
20	General maintenance tasks and considerations for the project see other files for
21	more specific topics. A chore in a chores file provides quick information on the
22	how and why of a particular chore.
23	
24	## Create a binary that lists jj info 
25	
26	This binary should list the changeID, commitID, and description title
27	and using `jj-lib`
28	```
29	
30	## Workflow and conventions
31	
32	Bot-facing workflow, versioning, and code conventions live in
33	[`../CLAUDE.md`](../CLAUDE.md). Start there for:
34	
35	- **Versioning during development** — single-step vs multi-step,
36	  `-N` pre-release suffixes, done-marker discipline.
37	- **Code Conventions** — doc comments on every file / fn / method,
38	  `// OK: …` justifications on `unwrap*` calls, ask-on-ambiguity,
39	  stuck detection.
40	- **Commit-Push-Finalize Flow** — two-checkpoint per-step
41	  discipline with hard stop after finalize.
42	
43	## Todo format
44	
45	Todo.md contains two main sections "Todo" and "Done" each item is a
46	short explanations of a tasks and links to more details using 1 or more
47	references.
48	
49	Todo items use lazy numbering — every entry begins with `1. ` and the
50	markdown renderer auto-numbers them. Reorder or insert items without
51	renumbering, and reference an entry by its displayed number ("let's
52	work on #3"). The Done section keeps `-` bullets — items aren't
53	referenced by number once completed.
54	
55	Multiple references must be separated: `[2],[3]` not `[2,3]` or `[2][3]`.
56	In markdown, `[2,3]` is a single ref key (won't resolve) and `[2][3]`
57	is parsed as display text `2` with ref key `3` (so `[2]` won't resolve).
58	
59	Examples:
60	
61	# Todo
62	- Add new feature X [details](features.md#feature-x)
63	- Fix bug Y [1]
64	
65	# Done
66	- Fixed issue Z [2],[3]
67	
68	[1]: bugs.md#bug-y
69	[2]: issues.md#issue-z
70	[3]: fixes.md#fix-z
71	
72	## Reference numbering
73	
74	Every note file (`todo.md`, `chores-NN.md`, `done.md`) keeps a
75	file-local `# References` section at the bottom. Reference numbers
76	are scoped to that file — `[1]` in `chores-07.md` and `[1]` in
77	`chores-01.md` are independent slots that may point at completely
78	different URLs. New chores files start their numbering at `[1]`.
79	
80	## Retiring Done entries
81	
82	`todo.md`'s `## Done` section is a rolling buffer of recently shipped
83	work, not a permanent log. Move entries into `done.md` at two natural
84	beats:
85	
86	- **Closing a ladder** — when the final `X.Y.Z` (no suffix) commit
87	  ships, decide which prior entries are no longer needed for nearby
88	  context and migrate them.
89	- **Opening a new ladder** — at `X.Y.Z-0`, do the same sweep before
90	  bumping the version.
91	
92	Migration mechanics:
93	
94	- Move the bullet itself from `todo.md > ## Done` to
95	  `done.md` (preserving the original ref number).
96	- Copy any references the moved entries cite into
97	  `done.md`'s `# References` section (those refs are file-local,
98	  so coexisting with `todo.md`'s namespace is fine).
99	- Prune any references in `todo.md > # References` no longer cited
100	  by anything in `## In Progress` / `## Todo` / `## Done`. This
101	  frees the numbers for future reuse.
102	
```

1	# Architecture
2	
3	How the `vc-x1` binary is structured: the clap-aware CLI
4	layer and the (emerging) clap-free subcommand layer.
5	
6	This is a living document; the app is under continuous
7	development and the repo will keep changing. Per-cycle design
8	discussion lives in `notes/chores-*.md`; near-term tasks in
9	`notes/todo.md`.
10	
11	## Overview
12	
13	`vc-x1` is a single binary that supports subcommands,
14	`vc-x1 <subcommand>`, for managing a jj-git **dual-repo workspace**
15	with an app repo plus a `.claude` bot-session repo. The commits
16	cross-reference each other via `ochid:` trailers at the bottom
17	of each commit message. See [`README.md`](README.md)
18	for the user-facing picture and [`CLAUDE.md`](CLAUDE.md) for
19	the bot workflow.
20	
21	## Two layers: CLI args vs subcommand Context + Params
22	
23	Goal: separate clap-aware argument parsing from subcommand
24	operation logic, so a future front-end (TUI, library
25	embedding) can call the same core without dragging clap
26	along. The trigger was `args.account.account`-style
27	nested-leaf access leaking into subcommand bodies; accessor
28	shortcuts were rejected as hiding the mismatch rather than
29	fixing it.
30	
31	- **CLI layer (clap-aware)**:
32	  Builds `XxxParams` from the user-facing `XxxArgs`
33	  `#[derive(Args)]` clap struct. Options/flags used in more
34	  than one subcommand live as flattened leaves in
35	  `src/options_flags/`.
36	- **Subcommand layer (clap-free)**:
37	  Entrypoint shape: `fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams) -> Result<…>`.
38	  And this isolates the subcommand from user and clap.
39	  - `Context` — common state shared with all subcommands.
40	  - `XxxParams` — a subcommand's parameters, derived from
41	    config or command-line arguments.
42	- **Boundary conversion**:
43	  Converting `XxxArgs` to `XxxParams` uses either
44	  `impl From<&XxxArgs> for XxxParams` or
45	  `impl TryFrom<&XxxArgs> for XxxParams`, depending on whether
46	  the conversion can fail.
47	
48	Two parameters per subcommand; the signature documents what
49	the subcommand depends on. `Context` is the same across all
50	subcommands; `XxxParams` carry the per-subcommand inputs.
51	
52	Rules for the subcommand layer (some still aspirational — see
53	[Migration A](#migration-a--args--context--params)):
54	
55	1. Plain options, flat fields, `Default` — no clap types in
56	   `XxxParams`. Domain types (`RepoSelector`, `Scope`) are
57	   fine; leaf wrappers (`RepoOption`) are not.
58	2. Typed errors (`enum XError`, not `Box<dyn Error>`) — a
59	   TUI matches variants to pick dialogs; CLI formats them.
60	3. Returned outcomes, not `println!` — each subcommand
61	   returns a structured result; CLI formats it, a library
62	   writes nothing to stdout itself.
63	4. Progress via an optional `&mut dyn ProgressSink` on
64	   long-running subcommands — CLI installs a stderr sink, tests a
65	   recording sink.
66	5. No globals, no implicit cwd/env reads in subcommands — CLI
67	   resolves cwd/env once at startup and builds `Context`.
68	
69	None of this is sacred — the whole structure is an experiment
70	and the repo keeps changing. The current scope is deliberately
71	narrow:
72	
73	- a small `Context` — two params, not one god blob;
74	- no trait-based DI yet;
75	- one crate, not a workspace split;
76	- `src/options_flags/` leaves left in place — only their
77	  consumers change shape.
78	
79	Widen any of it when the evidence (a second front-end, a second
80	consumer crate, a real platform-surface divergence) calls for
81	it — not before.
82	
83	### Naming
84	
85	Earlier drafts of this design (and the `notes/chores-09.md`
86	capture) used `Workspace` / `XOptions`. The implementation
87	(0.44.0) chose `Context` / `XxxParams` instead:
88	
89	- `Workspace` → `Context` — Cargo owns "workspace" formally,
90	  and this codebase already uses "workspace" for the
91	  dual-repo project root (`find_workspace_root`).
92	- `XOptions` → `XxxParams` — avoids visual collision with
93	  `Option<T>` (a params struct mixes required and
94	  `Option<T>`-typed fields) and with `src/options_flags/`.
95	
96	## Module map
97	
98	CLI layer:
99	
100	- `src/main.rs` — the clap edge:
101	  - `pub struct Cli` — clap `Parser`.
102	  - `pub enum Commands` — clap `Subcommand`; matched in the
103	    dispatch arms.
104	  - `cli_with_banner()` — walks the command tree adding the
105	    `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner as `before_help` on every subcommand.
106	  - `CompleteEnv` wiring for dynamic completion.
107	  - `bm_track` — a permanent `main`-bookmark tracking
108	    diagnostic printed on entry/exit of every command.
109	
110	- `src/options_flags/` — options/flags shared across
111	  subcommands, so a flag is defined once (parser, help,
112	  completer); Migration B tracks what's been moved here so
113	  far. See
114	  [`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md).
115	  - reusable **leaves**: `account`, `config`, `dry_run`,
116	    `private`, `push_retry`, `repo`, `scope`, `squash`,
117	    `use_template`.
118	  - reusable **bundles**: `provision_bundle`.
119	- `src/common.rs` — `CommonArgs` (the shared positional-rev /
120	  `-R` repo-list / `-n` / `--limit` / `-L` flag set flattened
121	  by `chid`, `desc`, `list`, `show`) plus the repo-iteration
122	  and revision-resolution helpers they share.
123	
124	Subcommand layer scaffolding:
125	
126	- `src/context.rs` — `Context`: the shared platform handle
127	  every subcommand runs against. Today it carries the loaded
128	  `UserConfig` and the `--log` path; built once at startup
129	  via `Context::load(log)`.
130	- `src/config.rs` — `UserConfig` (`~/.config/vc-x1/config.toml`).
131	- `src/scope.rs` — `Scope` enum (`Roles(Vec<Side>)` for the
132	  dual-repo `code`/`bot` roles, `Single(PathBuf)` for
133	  single-repo mode) and `parse_scope`.
134	
135	Subcommand modules — each holds an `XxxArgs` `#[derive(Args)]`
136	struct and a `pub fn x(...)` entrypoint:
137	
138	| Module | Subcommand | Notes |
139	| --- | --- | --- |
140	| `chid.rs` | `chid` | flattens `CommonArgs` |
141	| `desc.rs` (+ `desc_helpers.rs`) | `desc` | flattens `CommonArgs` |
142	| `list.rs` | `list` | flattens `CommonArgs` |
143	| `show.rs` | `show` | flattens `CommonArgs` |
144	| `validate_desc.rs` | `validate-desc` | migrated (0.48.0-4) — `validate_desc(&Context, &ValidateDescParams)`, `From<&ValidateDescArgs>` |
145	| `fix_desc.rs` | `fix-desc` | |
146	| `clone.rs` | `clone` | migrated (0.48.0-2) — `clone_repo(&Context, &CloneParams)`, `From<&CloneArgs>` |
147	| `init.rs` (+ `init/params.rs`) | `init` | subcommand-layer worked example (0.44.0) |
148	| `symlink.rs` | `symlink` | migrated (0.48.0-1) — `symlink(&Context, &SymlinkParams)`, `From<&SymlinkArgs>` |
149	| `sync.rs` | `sync` | migrated (0.48.0-3) — `sync(&Context, &SyncParams)`, `From<&SyncArgs>` |
150	| `finalize.rs` | `finalize` | migrated (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`, `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` |
151	| `push.rs` | `push` | resumable state machine |
152	
153	Support:
154	
155	- `src/repo_utils.rs`, `src/url.rs`, `src/toml_simple.rs`,
156	  `src/logging.rs` — jj/repo helpers, URL parsing, a minimal
157	  TOML reader, the CLI logger.
158	- `src/test_helpers.rs`, `src/test_tmp_root.rs` —
159	  `#[cfg(test)]` fixtures (`Fixture` / `FixturePor`) and
160	  tempdir resolution. Subcommand modules also carry their
161	  own `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` (and `integration_tests` for
162	  `sync` / `push`).
163	
164	## Subcommand model
165	
166	Adding a subcommand `x`:
167	
168	1. Create `src/x.rs` with `pub struct XxxArgs`
169	   (`#[derive(Args)]`), composing leaves from
170	   `src/options_flags/` where one exists.
171	2. Add a variant `X(x::XxxArgs)` to `Commands` in
172	   `src/main.rs` with a `///` summary (and
173	   `#[command(long_about = …)]` for the long form).
174	3. Add a dispatch arm in `main()` calling `x::x(&args)` (or,
175	   once ported, `x::x(&ctx, &params)`).
176	4. Doc comments on `#[arg(...)]` fields drive `--help`; add
177	   `#[arg(verbatim_doc_comment, …)]` on any field whose doc
178	   comment uses bullets (clap otherwise reflows them into
179	   prose).
180	5. Dynamic value completion (`--account=<TAB>`) is an
181	   `ArgValueCompleter` attached to the leaf in
182	   `src/options_flags/` — completion is a clap-aware concern
183	   and stays at this layer.
184	
185	## Migration A — args → Context + Params
186	
187	Port each subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to
188	`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)`, adding an
189	`XxxParams` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the
190	conversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0)
191	is the worked example; `finalize` (0.46.0) added the `TryFrom`
192	/ `Context.log` variant; the `0.48.0-N` cycle sweeps the rest.
193	Per-port status is the table below; the live checklist is the
194	"Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling" item in
195	[`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md).
196	
197	| Subcommand | Status |
198	| --- | --- |
199	| `init` | done (0.44.0) — `init(&Context, &InitParams)`; `From<&InitArgs>` |
200	| `finalize` | done (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`; `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` (fallible boundary); `--log` moved onto `Context` |
201	| `sync` | done (0.48.0-3) — `sync(&Context, &SyncParams)`; `From<&SyncArgs>` |
202	| `chid` | not started |
203	| `desc` | not started |
204	| `list` | not started |
205	| `show` | not started |
206	| `validate-desc` | done (0.48.0-4) — `validate_desc(&Context, &ValidateDescParams)`; `From<&ValidateDescArgs>` |
207	| `fix-desc` | not started |
208	| `clone` | done (0.48.0-2) — `clone_repo(&Context, &CloneParams)`; `From<&CloneArgs>` |
209	| `push` | not started |
210	| `symlink` | done (0.48.0-1) — `symlink(&Context, &SymlinkParams)`; `From<&SymlinkArgs>` |
211	
212	The rest are planned as one multi-step cycle (`0.48.0-N`), one
213	step per subcommand (`chid`/`desc`/`list`/`show` ride with the
214	separate "CommonArgs sweep" since their Migration A and B are
215	entangled).
216	
217	Out of scope for the early ports (deferred until a real
218	consumer surfaces): typed errors, returned outcomes vs
219	`println!`, the `ProgressSink`, and `Context` fields beyond
220	`UserConfig` + the `--log` path (`finalize` surfaced `log`).
221	
222	## Migration B — per-subcommand flags → `src/options_flags/`
223	
224	Independently of Migration A, per-subcommand inline `#[arg]`
225	fields are being lifted into reusable leaves/bundles under
226	`src/options_flags/` so a flag is defined (parser, help,
227	completer) exactly once. See
228	[`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md)
229	for the leaf / bundle / Pattern-A mechanics and the
230	Flag-vs-Option classification.
231	
232	State today:
233	
234	- **`init`** — fully composed from leaves/bundles
235	  (`account`, `repo`, `scope`, the `provision` bundle,
236	  `use_template`, `config`).
237	- **`chid` / `desc` / `list` / `show`** — share
238	  `common::CommonArgs`, a per-domain shared struct rather
239	  than an `options_flags/` leaf. The "CommonArgs sweep"
240	  todo item folds these into the leaf model (and drops the
241	  repeatable `-R`/`--repo` in favor of the `--scope` path
242	  form).
243	- **`finalize`** — `--squash` lifted to the `squash` leaf
244	  (which carries the `value`-field naming convention — see
245	  [`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md)
246	  "Adding a new leaf"). `--delay` / `--detach` / `--exec` /
247	  `--repo` / `--push` stay inline (no second consumer yet).
248	- **`sync` / `push` / `clone` / `validate-desc` /
249	  `fix-desc` / `symlink`** — still mostly inline `#[arg]`
250	  fields. The `--scope` retrofits queued in
251	  [`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md) are the usual entry
252	  point for converting one.
253	
254	## See also
255	
256	- [`README.md`](README.md) — user-facing overview and
257	  per-subcommand usage.
258	- [`CLAUDE.md`](CLAUDE.md) — bot workflow, versioning,
259	  commit/push conventions, code conventions.
260	- [`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md)
261	  — leaf/bundle patterns for Migration B.
262	- [`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md) — live task list,
263	  including both migrations.
264	- [`notes/chores-09.md`](notes/chores-09.md) — the
265	  0.43.0 / 0.44.0 design capture this document supersedes.
266	
```

[package]
name = "vc-x1"
version = "0.48.0-4"
edition = "2024"

[dependencies]
chrono = { version = "0.4", default-features = false, features = ["now"] }
clap = { version = "4.6", features = ["derive", "wrap_help"] }
clap_complete = { version = "4.6", features = ["unstable-dynamic"] }
jj-lib = "0.40"
log = { version = "0.4", features = ["std"] }
pollster = "0.4"

[target.'cfg(unix)'.dependencies]
libc = "0.2"
=== context.rs ===
//! Shared platform handle passed to every subcommand operation.
//!
//! `Context` holds platform state that is the same across every
//! subcommand: today, the loaded `UserConfig` and the resolved
//! `--log` path. Built once at CLI startup and threaded through
//! to the subcommand layer.
//!
//! The shape is deliberately minimal — fields like project root,
//! progress sinks, etc. are added when a real consumer surfaces.
//! See `ARCHITECTURE.md` for the CLI-args vs subcommand
//! Context+Params layering rationale.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use crate::config::{self, UserConfig};

/// Shared platform handle for subcommand operations.
///
/// - `user_config`: the loaded user config
///   (`~/.config/vc-x1/config.toml` or its discovered equivalent).
/// - `log`: the `--log` path, if given — where file-bound output
///   goes (and what a re-exec'd child is told to log to).
pub struct Context {
    pub user_config: UserConfig,
    pub log: Option<PathBuf>,
}

impl Context {
    /// Build a `Context` by loading the user config from disk and
    /// taking the CLI-resolved `--log` path.
    pub fn load(log: Option<PathBuf>) -> Result<Self, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        Ok(Self {
            user_config: config::load()?,
            log,
        })
    }
}
```

> AGENT

Oriented. Here's where things stand:

**Project:** `vc-x1` — a Rust CLI for managing a jj-git **dual-repo workspace** (app repo `.` + bot-session repo `.claude`), commits cross-referenced via `ochid:` trailers. Dual jj-managed repos; commit/push/finalize wrapped by `vc-x1 push <bookmark>`.

**Current state:** clean. `@` is empty on top of `main` at `aa0ee07 refactor: port validate-desc to Context (0.48.0-4)`. Cargo version `0.48.0-4`.

**Active cycle — Migration A sweep (`0.48.0-N`):** porting each subcommand `pub fn x(args)` → `pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)` with an `XxxParams` flat struct + `From`/`TryFrom<&XxxArgs>` at the binary edge.

Ladder (`notes/todo.md > ## In Progress`):
- `0.48.0-0` plan/version bump — done
- `0.48.0-1` symlink — done
- `0.48.0-2` clone — done
- `0.48.0-3` sync — done
- `0.48.0-4` validate-desc — done ← we are here
- `0.48.0-5` **fix-desc** — next

1	use std::path::PathBuf;
2	
3	use clap::Args;
4	use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
5	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
6	use log::{debug, info};
7	
8	use crate::common;
9	use crate::desc_helpers::{
10	    DEFAULT_ID_LEN, OchidIssues, TitleMatch, VC_CONFIG_FILE, append_ochid_trailer, extract_bare_id,
11	    extract_ochid_from_desc, find_matching_commit, fix_ochid_in_description,
12	    ochid_prefix_from_config, other_repo_from_config, resolve_full_change_id, validate_ochid,
13	};
14	use crate::toml_simple;
15	
16	/// Fix commit descriptions against the other repo.
17	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
18	pub struct FixDescArgs {
19	    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)
20	    #[arg(value_name = "REVISION")]
21	    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
22	
23	    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)
24	    #[arg(value_name = "COMMITS")]
25	    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
26	
27	    /// Revision(s) to scan
28	    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
29	    pub revision: String,
30	
31	    /// Number of commits to scan
32	    #[arg(short = 'n', long = "commits", value_name = "COMMITS")]
33	    pub limit: Option<usize>,
34	
35	    /// Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change [default: all]
36	    #[arg(short = 'm', long = "max-fixes")]
37	    pub max_fixes: Option<usize>,
38	
39	    /// Path to jj repo
40	    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
41	    pub repo: PathBuf,
42	
43	    /// Path to the other repo [default: from .vc-config.toml]
44	    #[arg(long = "other-repo")]
45	    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,
46	
47	    /// Expected changeID length
48	    #[arg(long = "id-len", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]
49	    pub id_len: usize,
50	
51	    /// New title to replace the first line (optional)
52	    #[arg(long)]
53	    pub title: Option<String>,
54	
55	    /// Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)
56	    #[arg(long)]
57	    pub fallback: Option<String>,
58	
59	    /// Actually write changes [default: dry-run]
60	    #[arg(long = "no-dry-run")]
61	    pub no_dry_run: bool,
62	
63	    /// Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp
64	    #[arg(long = "add-missing")]
65	    pub add_missing: bool,
66	}
67	
68	pub fn fix_desc(args: &FixDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
69	    debug!("fix-desc: enter");
70	    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
71	
72	    // Resolve other repo: --other-repo flag, or fall back to .vc-config.toml
73	    let other_repo_path = if let Some(ref p) = args.other_repo {
74	        p.clone()
75	    } else {
76	        let config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
77	        args.repo.join(other_repo_from_config(&config)?)
78	    };
79	
80	    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&other_repo_path)?;
81	    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&other_repo_path.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
82	    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
83	
84	    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
85	        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
86	        args.pos_count,
87	        &args.revision,
88	        args.limit,
89	        "@",
90	    );
91	
92	    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
93	        &workspace,
94	        &repo,
95	        &spec.rev,
96	        spec.desc_count,
97	        spec.anc_count,
98	    )?;
99	    if ids.is_empty() {
100	        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", args.revision).into());
101	    }
102	
103	    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
104	    let mut fixed = 0;
105	    let mut skipped = 0;
106	    let mut errors = 0;
107	
108	    for commit_id in &ids {
109	        if *commit_id == root_id {
110	            continue;
111	        }
112	        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
113	        let desc = commit.description();
114	        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
115	        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
116	        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: obvious
117	        let display_title = if first_line.is_empty() {
118	            "(no description set)"
119	        } else {
120	            first_line
121	        };
122	
123	        // Extract current ochid value
124	        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
125	
126	        // Validate current ochid
127	        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
128	            validate_ochid(
129	                ochid_val,
130	                &other_prefix,
131	                args.id_len,
132	                &other_workspace,
133	                &other_repo,
134	            )
135	        } else if args.add_missing {
136	            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
137	            if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
138	                && fixed >= max
139	            {
140	                skipped += 1;
141	                if !args.no_dry_run {
142	                    info!("skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)");
143	                }
144	                continue;
145	            }
146	            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo
147	            match find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)? {
148	                TitleMatch::NoTitle => {
149	                    skipped += 1;
150	                    if !args.no_dry_run {
151	                        info!("skip {change_short}  {display_title}");
152	                    }
153	                    continue;
154	                }
155	                TitleMatch::One(matched_id) => {
156	                    let new_desc =
157	                        append_ochid_trailer(desc, &other_prefix, &matched_id, args.id_len);
158	                    let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(args.id_len)];
159	                    if !args.no_dry_run {
160	                        info!("add  {change_short}  {display_title}  [missing]");
161	                        info!("     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}");
162	                    } else {
163	                        jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &args.repo, change_short)?;
164	                        info!("added {change_short}  {display_title}");
165	                    }
166	                    fixed += 1;
167	                    continue;
168	                }
169	                TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n) => {
170	                    skipped += 1;
171	                    if !args.no_dry_run {
172	                        info!(
173	                            "skip {change_short}  {display_title}  ({n} title matches, ambiguous)"
174	                        );
175	                    }
176	                    continue;
177	                }
178	                TitleMatch::None => {
179	                    skipped += 1;
180	                    if !args.no_dry_run {
181	                        info!(
182	                            "skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no matching title in other repo)"
183	                        );
184	                    }
185	                    continue;
186	                }
187	            }
188	        } else {
189	            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix
190	            skipped += 1;
191	            if !args.no_dry_run {
192	                info!("skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no ochid trailer)");
193	            }
194	            continue;
195	        };
196	
197	        if !issues.any() {
198	            skipped += 1;
199	            if !args.no_dry_run {
200	                info!("ok   {change_short}  {display_title}");
201	            }
202	            continue;
203	        }
204	
205	        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit
206	        if let Some(max) = args.max_fixes
207	            && fixed >= max
208	        {
209	            skipped += 1;
210	            if !args.no_dry_run {
211	                info!("skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)");
212	            }
213	            continue;
214	        }
215	
216	        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong
217	        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length.is_some() {
218	            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(""); // OK: obvious
219	            let bare_id = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);
220	            resolve_full_change_id(bare_id, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?
221	        } else {
222	            None
223	        };
224	
225	        // Build the fixed description
226	        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(
227	            desc,
228	            &other_prefix,
229	            args.id_len,
230	            args.title.as_deref(),
231	            resolved_id.as_deref(),
232	        );
233	
234	        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation
235	        let mut fixed_ochid = extract_ochid_from_desc(&new_desc);
236	
237	        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
238	            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, args.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)
239	        } else {
240	            OchidIssues {
241	                wrong_prefix: None,
242	                wrong_length: None,
243	                not_found: true,
244	                bare_id: String::new(),
245	            }
246	        };
247	
248	        if post_issues.not_found {
249	            if let Some(ref fallback) = args.fallback {
250	                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value
251	                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
252	                for line in &mut fb_lines {
253	                    if line.trim().starts_with("ochid:") {
254	                        *line = format!("ochid: {fallback}");
255	                    }
256	                }
257	                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\n') {
258	                    format!("{}\n", fb_lines.join("\n"))
259	                } else {
260	                    fb_lines.join("\n")
261	                };
262	                new_desc = fb_desc;
263	                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());
264	            } else {
265	                errors += 1;
266	                info!(
267	                    "err  {change_short}  {display_title}  (ID not found, ochid: {})",
268	                    fixed_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or("?") // OK: "?" placeholder when ochid unresolved
269	                );
270	                continue;
271	            }
272	        }
273	
274	        if !args.no_dry_run {
275	            info!(
276	                "fix  {change_short}  {display_title}  [{}]",
277	                issues.summary()
278	            );
279	            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {
280	                info!("     -> ochid: {v}");
281	            }
282	        } else {
283	            jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &args.repo, change_short)?;
284	            let fixed_title = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: obvious
285	            info!("fixed {change_short}  {fixed_title}");
286	        }
287	        fixed += 1;
288	    }
289	
290	    info!("");
291	    info!(
292	        "{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)",
293	        ids.len()
294	    );
295	    if errors > 0 {
296	        debug!("fix-desc: exit with errors");
297	        Err(format!("{errors} commit(s) could not be fixed").into())
298	    } else {
299	        debug!("fix-desc: exit");
300	        Ok(())
301	    }
302	}
303	
304	/// Run `jj describe` to rewrite a commit's description.
305	fn jj_describe(
306	    commit_id: &jj_lib::backend::CommitId,
307	    new_desc: &str,
308	    repo_path: &std::path::Path,
309	    _change_short: &str,
310	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
311	    crate::common::run(
312	        "jj",
313	        &[
314	            "describe",
315	            "-m",
316	            new_desc,
317	            "-r",
318	            &commit_id.hex(),
319	            "-R",
320	            &repo_path.to_string_lossy(),
321	            "--ignore-immutable",
322	        ],
323	        repo_path,
324	    )?;
325	    Ok(())
326	}
327	
```

1	//! The `validate-desc` subcommand: scan a revision range and check
2	//! each commit's `ochid:` trailer against the cross-referenced
3	//! repo — flagging missing, malformed, or dangling links.
4	//!
5	//! Read-only diagnostic; exits non-zero if any commit has issues.
6	//! The bot's "did the ochid wiring stay consistent?" check.
7	
8	use std::path::PathBuf;
9	
10	use clap::Args;
11	use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
12	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
13	
14	use log::{debug, info};
15	
16	use crate::common;
17	use crate::context::Context;
18	use crate::desc_helpers::{
19	    DEFAULT_ID_LEN, TitleMatch, VC_CONFIG_FILE, extract_bare_id, find_matching_commit,
20	    ochid_prefix_from_config, other_repo_from_config, validate_ochid,
21	};
22	use crate::toml_simple;
23	
24	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
25	pub struct ValidateDescArgs {
26	    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)
27	    #[arg(value_name = "REVISION")]
28	    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
29	
30	    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)
31	    #[arg(value_name = "COMMITS")]
32	    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
33	
34	    /// Revision(s) to scan
35	    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
36	    pub revision: String,
37	
38	    /// Number of commits to scan
39	    #[arg(short = 'n', long = "commits", value_name = "COMMITS")]
40	    pub limit: Option<usize>,
41	
42	    /// Path to jj repo
43	    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
44	    pub repo: PathBuf,
45	
46	    /// Path to the other repo [default: from .vc-config.toml]
47	    #[arg(long = "other-repo")]
48	    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,
49	
50	    /// Expected changeID length
51	    #[arg(long = "id-len", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]
52	    pub id_len: usize,
53	}
54	
55	/// Inputs to the validate-desc op, flat, owned, clap-free.
56	///
57	/// Mirrors `ValidateDescArgs`: positional `REVISION` / `COMMITS`
58	/// (`pos_rev` / `pos_count`), `--revision` / `--commits`
59	/// (`revision` / `limit`), `--repo`, `--other-repo`, `--id-len`.
60	pub struct ValidateDescParams {
61	    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
62	    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
63	    pub revision: String,
64	    pub limit: Option<usize>,
65	    pub repo: PathBuf,
66	    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,
67	    pub id_len: usize,
68	}
69	
70	impl From<&ValidateDescArgs> for ValidateDescParams {
71	    /// Convert clap-derived `ValidateDescArgs` into the flat
72	    /// `ValidateDescParams` (total — every field copies straight over).
73	    fn from(a: &ValidateDescArgs) -> Self {
74	        Self {
75	            pos_rev: a.pos_rev.clone(),
76	            pos_count: a.pos_count,
77	            revision: a.revision.clone(),
78	            limit: a.limit,
79	            repo: a.repo.clone(),
80	            other_repo: a.other_repo.clone(),
81	            id_len: a.id_len,
82	        }
83	    }
84	}
85	
86	/// Status of a single commit's ochid validation.
87	enum CommitStatus {
88	    Ok,
89	    Lost,                     // ochid marked as "lost" (unrecoverable)
90	    None_,                    // ochid marked as "none" (no counterpart)
91	    NeedsFixed(String),       // summary of issues
92	    MissingNoTitle,           // no ochid, no title to match on
93	    MissingNoMatch,           // no ochid, no title match in other repo
94	    MissingAmbiguous(usize),  // no ochid, multiple title matches
95	    MissingWithMatch(String), // no ochid, unique title match found
96	}
97	
98	/// Run the `validate-desc` subcommand: scan the resolved revision
99	/// range and report each commit's ochid status against the other
100	/// repo; errors if any commit has issues.
101	///
102	/// `ctx` is unused today (validate-desc reads `.vc-config.toml`,
103	/// not the user config, and doesn't touch the `--log` path); it's
104	/// present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.
105	pub fn validate_desc(
106	    _ctx: &Context,
107	    params: &ValidateDescParams,
108	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
109	    debug!("validate-desc: enter");
110	    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&params.repo)?;
111	
112	    // Resolve other repo: --other-repo flag, or fall back to .vc-config.toml
113	    let other_repo_path = if let Some(ref p) = params.other_repo {
114	        p.clone()
115	    } else {
116	        let config = toml_simple::toml_load(&params.repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
117	        params.repo.join(other_repo_from_config(&config)?)
118	    };
119	
120	    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&other_repo_path)?;
121	    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&other_repo_path.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
122	    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
123	
124	    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
125	        params.pos_rev.as_deref(),
126	        params.pos_count,
127	        &params.revision,
128	        params.limit,
129	        "@",
130	    );
131	
132	    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
133	        &workspace,
134	        &repo,
135	        &spec.rev,
136	        spec.desc_count,
137	        spec.anc_count,
138	    )?;
139	    if ids.is_empty() {
140	        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", params.revision).into());
141	    }
142	
143	    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
144	    let mut valid = 0;
145	    let mut lost = 0;
146	    let mut none = 0;
147	    let mut issues_count = 0;
148	    let mut missing = 0;
149	
150	    let col_header = "STAT CHANGEID      TITLE";
151	    info!("{col_header}");
152	
153	    for commit_id in &ids {
154	        if *commit_id == root_id {
155	            continue;
156	        }
157	        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
158	        let desc = commit.description();
159	        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
160	        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
161	        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: obvious
162	        let display_title = if first_line.is_empty() {
163	            "(no description set)"
164	        } else {
165	            first_line
166	        };
167	
168	        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
169	
170	        let status = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
171	            let bare = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);
172	            if bare == "lost" {
173	                CommitStatus::Lost
174	            } else if bare == "none" {
175	                CommitStatus::None_
176	            } else {
177	                let issues = validate_ochid(
178	                    ochid_val,
179	                    &other_prefix,
180	                    params.id_len,
181	                    &other_workspace,
182	                    &other_repo,
183	                );
184	                if issues.any() {
185	                    CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(issues.summary())
186	                } else {
187	                    CommitStatus::Ok
188	                }
189	            }
190	        } else {
191	            match find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)? {
192	                TitleMatch::NoTitle => CommitStatus::MissingNoTitle,
193	                TitleMatch::One(id) => {
194	                    let short_id = &id[..id.len().min(params.id_len)];
195	                    CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(format!("{other_prefix}{short_id}"))
196	                }
197	                TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n) => CommitStatus::MissingAmbiguous(n),
198	                TitleMatch::None => CommitStatus::MissingNoMatch,
199	            }
200	        };
201	
202	        match status {
203	            CommitStatus::Ok => {
204	                valid += 1;
205	                info!("ok   {change_short}  {display_title}");
206	            }
207	            CommitStatus::Lost => {
208	                lost += 1;
209	                info!("lost {change_short}  {display_title}");
210	            }
211	            CommitStatus::None_ => {
212	                none += 1;
213	                info!("none {change_short}  {display_title}");
214	            }
215	            CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(summary) => {
216	                issues_count += 1;
217	                info!("err  {change_short}  {display_title}  [{summary}]");
218	            }
219	            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(ochid) => {
220	                missing += 1;
221	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [match: {ochid}]");
222	            }
223	            CommitStatus::MissingNoTitle => {
224	                missing += 1;
225	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}");
226	            }
227	            CommitStatus::MissingAmbiguous(n) => {
228	                missing += 1;
229	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [{n} title matches, ambiguous]");
230	            }
231	            CommitStatus::MissingNoMatch => {
232	                missing += 1;
233	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [no matching title in other repo]");
234	            }
235	        }
236	    }
237	
238	    let total = valid + lost + none + issues_count + missing;
239	    if total > 10 {
240	        info!("{col_header}");
241	    }
242	    info!("");
243	    info!(
244	        "{valid} valid, {lost} lost, {none} none, {issues_count} issues, {missing} missing (of {} total)",
245	        ids.len()
246	    );
247	    if issues_count > 0 {
248	        debug!("validate-desc: exit with issues");
249	        Err(format!("{issues_count} commit(s) have issues").into())
250	    } else {
251	        debug!("validate-desc: exit");
252	        Ok(())
253	    }
254	}
255

  "content": "//! The `fix-desc` subcommand: scan a revision range and repair\n//! each commit's `ochid:` trailer against the cross-referenced\n//! repo — fixing wrong prefixes/lengths, adding missing trailers\n//! (`--add-missing`), or substituting a fallback value.\n//!\n//! Dry-run by default; `--no-dry-run` actually rewrites\n//! descriptions via `jj describe --ignore-immutable`.\n\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common;\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::desc_helpers::{\n    DEFAULT_ID_LEN, OchidIssues, TitleMatch, VC_CONFIG_FILE, append_ochid_trailer, extract_bare_id,\n    extract_ochid_from_desc, find_matching_commit, fix_ochid_in_description,\n    ochid_prefix_from_config, other_repo_from_config, resolve_full_change_id, validate_ochid,\n};\nuse crate::toml_simple;\n\n/// Fix commit descriptions against the other repo.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct FixDescArgs {\n    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"REVISION\")]\n    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)\n    #[arg(value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Revision(s) to scan\n    #[arg(short, long, default_value = \"@\")]\n    pub revision: String,\n\n    /// Number of commits to scan\n    #[arg(short = 'n', long = \"commits\", value_name = \"COMMITS\")]\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Maximum number of commits to actually fix/change [default: all]\n    #[arg(short = 'm', long = \"max-fixes\")]\n    pub max_fixes: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Path to jj repo\n    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = \".\")]\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Path to the other repo [default: from .vc-config.toml]\n    #[arg(long = \"other-repo\")]\n    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// Expected changeID length\n    #[arg(long = \"id-len\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]\n    pub id_len: usize,\n\n    /// New title to replace the first line (optional)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Fallback ochid value for IDs not found in other repo (e.g. /.claude/lost)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub fallback: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Actually write changes [default: dry-run]\n    #[arg(long = \"no-dry-run\")]\n    pub no_dry_run: bool,\n\n    /// Add missing ochid trailers by matching title and timestamp\n    #[arg(long = \"add-missing\")]\n    pub add_missing: bool,\n}\n\n/// Inputs to the fix-desc op, flat, owned, clap-free.\n///\n/// Mirrors `FixDescArgs` field-for-field: positional `REVISION` /\n/// `COMMITS` (`pos_rev` / `pos_count`), `--revision` / `--commits`\n/// (`revision` / `limit`), `--max-fixes`, `--repo`, `--other-repo`,\n/// `--id-len`, `--title`, `--fallback`, `--no-dry-run`,\n/// `--add-missing`.\npub struct FixDescParams {\n    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,\n    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,\n    pub revision: String,\n    pub limit: Option<usize>,\n    pub max_fixes: Option<usize>,\n    pub repo: PathBuf,\n    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,\n    pub id_len: usize,\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n    pub fallback: Option<String>,\n    pub no_dry_run: bool,\n    pub add_missing: bool,\n}\n\nimpl From<&FixDescArgs> for FixDescParams {\n    /// Convert clap-derived `FixDescArgs` into the flat\n    /// `FixDescParams` (total — every field copies straight over).\n    fn from(a: &FixDescArgs) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            pos_rev: a.pos_rev.clone(),\n            pos_count: a.pos_count,\n            revision: a.revision.clone(),\n            limit: a.limit,\n            max_fixes: a.max_fixes,\n            repo: a.repo.clone(),\n            other_repo: a.other_repo.clone(),\n            id_len: a.id_len,\n            title: a.title.clone(),\n            fallback: a.fallback.clone(),\n            no_dry_run: a.no_dry_run,\n            add_missing: a.add_missing,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Run the `fix-desc` subcommand: scan the resolved revision range\n/// and repair each commit's ochid trailer against the other repo;\n/// errors if any commit could not be fixed.\n///\n/// `ctx` is unused today (fix-desc reads `.vc-config.toml`, not the\n/// user config, and doesn't touch the `--log` path); it's present\n/// for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.\npub fn fix_desc(_ctx: &Context, params: &FixDescParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"fix-desc: enter\");\n    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&params.repo)?;\n\n    // Resolve other repo: --other-repo flag, or fall back to .vc-config.toml\n    let other_repo_path = if let Some(ref p) = params.other_repo {\n        p.clone()\n    } else {\n        let config = toml_simple::toml_load(&params.repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n        params.repo.join(other_repo_from_config(&config)?)\n    };\n\n    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&other_repo_path)?;\n    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&other_repo_path.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;\n    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(\n        params.pos_rev.as_deref(),\n        params.pos_count,\n        &params.revision,\n        params.limit,\n        \"@\",\n    );\n\n    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(\n        &workspace,\n        &repo,\n        &spec.rev,\n        spec.desc_count,\n        spec.anc_count,\n    )?;\n    if ids.is_empty() {\n        return Err(format!(\"no commits found for revision '{}'\", params.revision).into());\n    }\n\n    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();\n    let mut fixed = 0;\n    let mut skipped = 0;\n    let mut errors = 0;\n\n    for commit_id in &ids {\n        if *commit_id == root_id {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n        let desc = commit.description();\n        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());\n        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];\n        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\"); // OK: obvious\n        let display_title = if first_line.is_empty() {\n            \"(no description set)\"\n        } else {\n            first_line\n        };\n\n        // Extract current ochid value\n        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);\n\n        // Validate current ochid\n        let issues = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(\n                ochid_val,\n                &other_prefix,\n                params.id_len,\n                &other_workspace,\n                &other_repo,\n            )\n        } else if params.add_missing {\n            // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n            if let Some(max) = params.max_fixes\n                && fixed >= max\n            {\n                skipped += 1;\n                if !params.no_dry_run {\n                    info!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n            // No ochid trailer — try to infer from the other repo\n            match find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)? {\n                TitleMatch::NoTitle => {\n                    skipped += 1;\n                    if !params.no_dry_run {\n                        info!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n                    }\n                    continue;\n                }\n                TitleMatch::One(matched_id) => {\n                    let new_desc =\n                        append_ochid_trailer(desc, &other_prefix, &matched_id, params.id_len);\n                    let short_matched = &matched_id[..matched_id.len().min(params.id_len)];\n                    if !params.no_dry_run {\n                        info!(\"add  {change_short}  {display_title}  [missing]\");\n                        info!(\"     -> ochid: {other_prefix}{short_matched}\");\n                    } else {\n                        jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &params.repo, change_short)?;\n                        info!(\"added {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n                    }\n                    fixed += 1;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n) => {\n                    skipped += 1;\n                    if !params.no_dry_run {\n                        info!(\n                            \"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  ({n} title matches, ambiguous)\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                    continue;\n                }\n                TitleMatch::None => {\n                    skipped += 1;\n                    if !params.no_dry_run {\n                        info!(\n                            \"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no matching title in other repo)\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                    continue;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            // No ochid trailer — nothing to fix\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !params.no_dry_run {\n                info!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (no ochid trailer)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        };\n\n        if !issues.any() {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !params.no_dry_run {\n                info!(\"ok   {change_short}  {display_title}\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Stop if we've hit the max-fixes limit\n        if let Some(max) = params.max_fixes\n            && fixed >= max\n        {\n            skipped += 1;\n            if !params.no_dry_run {\n                info!(\"skip {change_short}  {display_title}  (max-fixes reached)\");\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Resolve the full change ID from the other repo when length is wrong\n        let resolved_id = if issues.wrong_length.is_some() {\n            let ochid_val = current_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"); // OK: obvious\n            let bare_id = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);\n            resolve_full_change_id(bare_id, &other_workspace, &other_repo)?\n        } else {\n            None\n        };\n\n        // Build the fixed description\n        let mut new_desc = fix_ochid_in_description(\n            desc,\n            &other_prefix,\n            params.id_len,\n            params.title.as_deref(),\n            resolved_id.as_deref(),\n        );\n\n        // Re-extract the fixed ochid value for post-validation\n        let mut fixed_ochid = extract_ochid_from_desc(&new_desc);\n\n        let post_issues = if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n            validate_ochid(v, &other_prefix, params.id_len, &other_workspace, &other_repo)\n        } else {\n            OchidIssues {\n                wrong_prefix: None,\n                wrong_length: None,\n                not_found: true,\n                bare_id: String::new(),\n            }\n        };\n\n        if post_issues.not_found {\n            if let Some(ref fallback) = params.fallback {\n                // Replace the ochid line with the fallback value\n                let mut fb_lines: Vec<String> = new_desc.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();\n                for line in &mut fb_lines {\n                    if line.trim().starts_with(\"ochid:\") {\n                        *line = format!(\"ochid: {fallback}\");\n                    }\n                }\n                let fb_desc = if new_desc.ends_with('\\n') {\n                    format!(\"{}\\n\", fb_lines.join(\"\\n\"))\n                } else {\n                    fb_lines.join(\"\\n\")\n                };\n                new_desc = fb_desc;\n                fixed_ochid = Some(fallback.clone());\n            } else {\n                errors += 1;\n                info!(\n                    \"err  {change_short}  {display_title}  (ID not found, ochid: {})\",\n                    fixed_ochid.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"?\") // OK: \"?\" placeholder when ochid unresolved\n                );\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if !params.no_dry_run {\n            info!(\n                \"fix  {change_short}  {display_title}  [{}]\",\n                issues.summary()\n            );\n            if let Some(ref v) = fixed_ochid {\n                info!(\"     -> ochid: {v}\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            jj_describe(commit_id, &new_desc, &params.repo, change_short)?;\n            let fixed_title = new_desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\"); // OK: obvious\n            info!(\"fixed {change_short}  {fixed_title}\");\n        }\n        fixed += 1;\n    }\n\n    info!(\"\");\n    info!(\n        \"{fixed} fixed, {skipped} skipped, {errors} errors (of {} total)\",\n        ids.len()\n    );\n    if errors > 0 {\n        debug!(\"fix-desc: exit with errors\");\n        Err(format!(\"{errors} commit(s) could not be fixed\").into())\n    } else {\n        debug!(\"fix-desc: exit\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Run `jj describe` to rewrite a commit's description.\nfn jj_describe(\n    commit_id: &jj_lib::backend::CommitId,\n    new_desc: &str,\n    repo_path: &std::path::Path,\n    _change_short: &str,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    crate::common::run(\n        \"jj\",\n        &[\n            \"describe\",\n            \"-m\",\n            new_desc,\n            \"-r\",\n            &commit_id.hex(),\n            \"-R\",\n            &repo_path.to_string_lossy(),\n            \"--ignore-immutable\",\n        ],\n        repo_path,\n    )?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n"
}

120	///
121	/// Flag set mirrors the design in `notes/chores-05.md`. Flags are
122	/// parsed in 0.37.0-0; their real effects land across the remaining
123	/// 0.37.0-N steps. See the module docstring for which flags activate
124	/// when.
125	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
126	pub struct PushArgs {
127	    /// Bookmark to advance in both repos (positional form of `--bookmark`).
128	    ///
129	    /// Accepting a positional lets the common case read as `vc-x1 push main`
130	    /// without the `--bookmark` ceremony; `--bookmark` is kept as an
131	    /// alias for scripts and for composition with other args. The two
132	    /// forms conflict if both supplied.
133	    #[arg(value_name = "BOOKMARK", conflicts_with = "bookmark")]
134	    pub bookmark_pos: Option<String>,
135	
136	    /// Bookmark to advance in both repos (flag form; see positional).
137	    #[arg(long, conflicts_with = "bookmark_pos")]
138	    pub bookmark: Option<String>,
139	
140	    /// Clear any saved state file and start from stage 1.
141	    #[arg(long)]
142	    pub restart: bool,
143	
144	    /// Explicit stage to jump to (advanced / debug use).
145	    #[arg(long, value_name = "STAGE")]
146	    pub from: Option<Stage>,
147	
148	    /// Pause between every stage for an interactive approval gate.
149	    #[arg(long)]
150	    pub step: bool,
151	
152	    /// Print where the saved state thinks we are and exit.
153	    #[arg(long)]
154	    pub status: bool,
155	
156	    /// Re-run `preflight` even on resume (default: skip if last run succeeded).
157	    #[arg(long)]
158	    pub recheck: bool,
159	
160	    /// Stop before `finalize-claude` so it can be run manually.
161	    #[arg(long)]
162	    pub no_finalize: bool,
163	
164	    /// Print the exact commands for every stage without side effects.
165	    #[arg(long)]
166	    pub dry_run: bool,
167	
168	    /// Commit title (skip `$EDITOR` for the message stage).
169	    #[arg(long, value_name = "STR")]
170	    pub title: Option<String>,
171	
172	    /// Commit body (skip `$EDITOR` for the message stage).
173	    #[arg(long, value_name = "STR")]
174	    pub body: Option<String>,
175	
176	    /// Auto-approve interactive prompts (review gate, message-edit
177	    /// confirmation). Required when `--title` / `--body` aren't both
178	    /// supplied in non-interactive contexts.
179	    #[arg(short = 'y', long)]
180	    pub yes: bool,
181	}
182	
183	/// Current state-file format version — bump when the flat key set
184	/// changes incompatibly so readers can detect stale state and refuse
185	/// to resume instead of silently misinterpreting fields.
186	const STATE_FORMAT_VERSION: u32 = 1;
187	
188	/// Default directory (relative to app-repo root) for the push state
189	/// file when `.vc-config.toml` has no `[push]` override.
190	const DEFAULT_STATE_DIR: &str = ".vc-x1";
191	
192	/// Default filename for the push state file under `state_dir`.
193	const DEFAULT_STATE_FILE: &str = "push-state.toml";
194	
195	/// Resolved state-file layout — just the full path today, structured
196	/// so future additions (e.g. a `dir` exposed for `.gitignore`
197	/// coherence checks in 0.37.0-3) have a place to land without
198	/// changing callers.
199	#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
200	pub struct StateLayout {
201	    /// Full path `<repo>/<state-dir>/<state-file>`.
202	    pub path: PathBuf,
203	}
204	
205	/// Read `state_dir` / `state_file` from `<repo_root>/.vc-config.toml`
206	/// under the `[push]` section, falling back to defaults (`.vc-x1`
207	/// and `push-state.toml`).
208	///
209	/// Missing config file is not an error — every workspace has the
210	/// defaults applied silently so `vc-x1 push --status` works on a
211	/// fresh clone without ceremony.
212	pub fn resolve_state_layout(repo_root: &Path) -> StateLayout {
213	    let config_path = repo_root.join(".vc-config.toml");
214	    let (dir, file) = if config_path.exists() {
215	        match toml_load(&config_path) {
216	            Ok(map) => {
217	                let dir = map
218	                    .get("push.state-dir")
219	                    .cloned()
220	                    .unwrap_or_else(|| DEFAULT_STATE_DIR.to_string());
221	                let file = map
222	                    .get("push.state-file")
223	                    .cloned()
224	                    .unwrap_or_else(|| DEFAULT_STATE_FILE.to_string());
225	                (dir, file)
226	            }
227	            Err(e) => {
228	                debug!("push: ignoring unparseable .vc-config.toml: {e}");
229	                (
230	                    DEFAULT_STATE_DIR.to_string(),
231	                    DEFAULT_STATE_FILE.to_string(),
232	                )
233	            }
234	        }
235	    } else {
236	        (
237	            DEFAULT_STATE_DIR.to_string(),
238	            DEFAULT_STATE_FILE.to_string(),
239	        )
240	    };
241	    let path = repo_root.join(&dir).join(&file);
242	    StateLayout { path }
243	}
244	
245	/// Persistent state for an in-progress `push` run.
246	///
247	/// Serialized as flat TOML (`key = "value"`) under the dir/file
248	/// configured in `.vc-config.toml`'s `[push]` section. Fields added
249	/// across dev steps are all `Option<_>` so older states remain
250	/// loadable — only `version` / `stage` / `bookmark` / `started_at`
251	/// are required. See the module docstring for which dev step
252	/// introduced which field.
253	#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
254	pub struct PushState {
255	    /// State-file format version; must match `STATE_FORMAT_VERSION`
256	    /// to be considered readable.
257	    pub version: u32,
258	    /// The next stage to execute on resume.
259	    pub stage: Stage,
260	    /// Bookmark being advanced by this run (persisted so resume
261	    /// doesn't need `--bookmark` again).
262	    pub bookmark: String,
263	    /// ISO-8601 UTC timestamp captured when the state was first
264	    /// written. Informational — helps the user spot stale state.
265	    pub started_at: String,
266	    /// App-repo changeID captured at `message` stage (before
267	    /// `commit-app` runs). Stable across `jj commit` — becomes the
268	    /// chid of the just-committed change. Used when composing the
269	    /// `.claude` commit's ochid trailer. Added in 0.37.0-2.
270	    pub app_chid: Option<String>,
271	    /// `.claude` repo changeID used by the app-repo commit's ochid
272	    /// trailer. Either the pre-commit `@` chid (when `.claude` has
273	    /// pending changes — becomes `@-` after commit) or the current
274	    /// `@-` chid (when `.claude` is clean — stays stable). Added in
275	    /// 0.37.0-2.
276	    pub claude_chid: Option<String>,
277	    /// Whether `.claude`'s working copy had changes at `message`
278	    /// time. Decides whether `commit-claude` actually runs or
279	    /// skips. Added in 0.37.0-2.
280	    pub claude_had_changes: Option<bool>,
281	    /// `jj op` id of the app repo captured before `commit-app`. On
282	    /// failure in stages 4-6, `jj op restore` rewinds here. Added
283	    /// in 0.37.0-2.
284	    pub op_app: Option<String>,
285	    /// `jj op` id of `.claude` captured before `commit-app`. Same
286	    /// rollback target as `op_app`. Added in 0.37.0-2.
287	    pub op_claude: Option<String>,
288	    /// Composed commit title — persisted so resume doesn't need
289	    /// `--title` re-passed. Set during `message` stage from either
290	    /// `--title` or `$EDITOR`. Added in 0.37.0-4.
291	    pub title: Option<String>,
292	    /// Composed commit body (sans ochid trailer, which each commit
293	    /// stage appends). Persisted alongside title. Multi-line
294	    /// content is escaped for the flat-TOML save format (see
295	    /// `escape_multiline` / `unescape_multiline`). Added in
296	    /// 0.37.0-4.
297	    pub body: Option<String>,
298	}
299	
300	impl PushState {
301	    /// Build a fresh state for a new run.
302	    pub fn new_for(bookmark: &str) -> Self {
303	        PushState {
304	            version: STATE_FORMAT_VERSION,
305	            stage: Stage::first(),
306	            bookmark: bookmark.to_string(),
307	            started_at: Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
308	            app_chid: None,
309	            claude_chid: None,
310	            claude_had_changes: None,
311	            op_app: None,
312	            op_claude: None,
313	            title: None,
314	            body: None,
315	        }
316	    }
317	
318	    /// Write the state to `path`, creating parent dirs as needed.
319	    ///
320	    /// Values are single-line and contain no `"` characters under
321	    /// normal operation (stage names are kebab-case, chids are
322	    /// ASCII, bookmark names don't contain quotes, timestamps are
323	    /// ASCII). A defensive escape pass replaces any stray `"` with
324	    /// `\"` so the file always parses with `toml_simple`. Optional
325	    /// fields are only emitted when set so older state files don't
326	    /// carry a wall of blank keys.
327	    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
328	        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
329	            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
330	        }
331	        let mut content = String::new();
332	        content.push_str("# vc-x1 push state — managed file, do not edit by hand\n");
333	        content.push_str("[push-state]\n");
334	        content.push_str(&format!("version = {}\n", self.version));

534	pub fn push(args: &PushArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
535	    let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
536	    push_in(&cwd, args)
537	}
538	
539	/// `push` parameterized on the workspace root. CLI dispatch calls
540	/// this with `std::env::current_dir()`; integration tests call it
541	/// with a fixture tempdir so the stage bodies mutate the fixture's
542	/// repos instead of the developer's working tree.
543	pub(crate) fn push_in(
544	    workspace_root: &Path,
545	    args: &PushArgs,
546	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
547	    let layout = resolve_state_layout(workspace_root);
548	
549	    if args.status {
550	        return cmd_status(&layout);
551	    }
552	
553	    if args.dry_run {
554	        info!("push: DRY-RUN — no side effects (no commits, no pushes, no state written)");
555	    }
556	
557	    // Gitignore coherence check (fatal). Only checked when we
558	    // actually have a file path with a parent (resolve_state_layout
559	    // always gives one, so this is effectively always-on).
560	    if let Some(dir_name) = layout
561	        .path
562	        .parent()
563	        .and_then(|p| p.file_name())
564	        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
565	    {
566	        check_gitignore_coherence(workspace_root, dir_name)?;
567	    }
568	
569	    if args.restart && layout.path.exists() {
570	        fs::remove_file(&layout.path)?;
571	        debug!("push: --restart cleared state at {}", layout.path.display());
572	    }
573	
574	    // Load existing state, or require a bookmark (positional or flag)
575	    // to bootstrap one.
576	    let mut state = match PushState::load(&layout.path)? {
577	        Some(s) => s,
578	        None => {
579	            let bookmark = args
580	                .bookmark_pos
581	                .as_deref()
582	                .or(args.bookmark.as_deref())
583	                .ok_or(
584	                    "push: no saved state; a bookmark is required to start a new run \
585	                     — pass it as a positional (`vc-x1 push main`) or via `--bookmark main`",
586	                )?;
587	            PushState::new_for(bookmark)
588	        }
589	    };
590	
591	    // `--from` overrides the resumed stage (does not affect bookmark
592	    // or other persisted fields).
593	    if let Some(from) = args.from {
594	        state.stage = from;
595	    }
596	
597	    run_from(workspace_root, &mut state, args, &layout)
598	}
599	
600	/// Full path of the `.claude` session repo for a given workspace
601	/// root. Centralized so a future layout change (e.g. configurable
602	/// session-repo name) has one caller to update.
603	fn claude_path(workspace_root: &Path) -> PathBuf {
604	    workspace_root.join(".claude")
605	}
606	
607	/// Print the resumed stage (or "no saved state") and return.
608	fn cmd_status(layout: &StateLayout) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
609	    match PushState::load(&layout.path)? {
610	        Some(state) => {
611	            info!(
612	                "push-state: stage={} bookmark={} started={} (file: {})",
613	                state.stage.as_str(),
614	                state.bookmark,
615	                state.started_at,
616	                layout.path.display()
617	            );
618	        }
619	        None => {
620	            info!(
621	                "push-state: no saved state ({} does not exist)",
622	                layout.path.display()
623	            );
624	        }
625	    }
626	    Ok(())
627	}
628	
629	/// Walk the state machine from `state.stage` to the end, saving
630	/// progress after each stage.
631	///
632	/// Records a `jj op` snapshot in both repos the first time we enter
633	/// `commit-app` and leaves it in state so resume inherits the
634	/// rollback target. On failure inside the rollback-eligible window
635	/// (`commit-app` / `commit-claude` / `bookmark-both`), both repos
636	/// are restored before the error propagates.
637	fn run_from(
638	    root: &Path,
639	    state: &mut PushState,
640	    args: &PushArgs,
641	    layout: &StateLayout,
642	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
643	    // State-sanity preflight: catch stale-state-after-out-of-band-recovery
644	    // (no-op on fresh state). Runs before save so a bogus halt point isn't
645	    // re-persisted. Per chores-06 [61] design.
646	    verify_state_sanity(root, state)?;
647	
648	    // Only persist state in real runs. Dry-runs are inspection-only
649	    // — carrying their inferred chids / op snapshots into real runs
650	    // would mislead the user about what was actually recorded.
651	    if !args.dry_run {
652	        state.save(&layout.path)?;
653	    }
654	    loop {
655	        let stage = state.stage;
656	
657	        // Snapshot op ids once on first `commit-app` entry. Skipped
658	        // in dry-run since we won't mutate anything to roll back.
659	        if stage == Stage::CommitApp && state.op_app.is_none() && !args.dry_run {
660	            state.op_app = Some(current_op_id(root)?);
661	            state.op_claude = Some(current_op_id(&claude_path(root))?);
662	            state.save(&layout.path)?;
663	        }

=== finalize stage ===
fn stage_finalize_claude(
    root: &Path,
    state: &PushState,
    args: &PushArgs,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    if args.no_finalize {
        info!("push finalize-claude: skip (--no-finalize)");
        return Ok(());
    }
    let bk = &state.bookmark;
    let claude = claude_path(root);
    let claude_arg = claude.to_string_lossy();
    if args.dry_run {
        info!(
            "push finalize-claude: [dry-run] would run vc-x1 finalize --repo {claude_arg} --squash --push {bk} --delay 10 --detach"
        );
        return Ok(());
    }
    info!(
        "push finalize-claude: vc-x1 finalize --repo {claude_arg} --squash --push {bk} --delay 10 --detach"
    );
    run(
        "vc-x1",
        &[
            "finalize",
            "--repo",
            &claude_arg,
            "--squash",
            "--push",
            bk,
            "--delay",
            "10",
            "--detach",
            "--log",
            "/tmp/vc-x1-finalize.log",
        ],
        root,
    )?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Verify the saved state still matches reality before any stage runs.
///
/// Catches the failure mode where out-of-band activity (manual finalize,
/// squash + force-push, abandon, etc.) moved the world forward between
/// the prior push and this resume, leaving `state` pointing at a
/// now-bogus halt point. The 0.37.1 dogfood incident is the canonical
/// example: push parked at `finalize-claude` after a failure; manual
/// recovery moved the world forward; the next push resumed at the
/// parked stage, no-op'd, and falsely declared "completed all stages"
/// while working copies still held uncommitted changes.
///
/// Three checks (per chores-06 [61] design):
///
/// 1. `state.app_chid` still resolves in the app repo (not abandoned).
/// 2. After `bookmark-both` has run (state.stage ∈ {PushApp,
///    FinalizeClaude}), the bookmark points at a commit whose chid
///    matches `state.app_chid`.
/// 3. `state.claude_chid` still resolves in `.claude`.
///
/// On any mismatch: error with the exact `vc-x1 push <bookmark>
/// --restart` remediation. A fresh state (no chids yet) is a no-op —
/// nothing to verify.
```

18	/// Build a unique tempdir for a test and create it.
19	fn unique_tmp(tag: &str) -> PathBuf {
20	    let ts = SystemTime::now()
21	        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
22	        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
23	        .unwrap_or(0); // OK: clock error → 0 is harmless for unique tempdir naming
24	    let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
25	    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("vc-x1-push-{tag}-{ts}-{n}"));

27	    path
28	}

=== config::load ===
pub fn load() -> Result<UserConfig, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    load_from(&config_path()?)
}
pub fn load_from(path: &Path) -> Result<UserConfig, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    if !path.exists() {
        debug!(
            "config: file not found at {} — using empty UserConfig",
            path.display()
        );
        return Ok(UserConfig::default());
    }
    debug!("config: loading from {}", path.display());
    let map = toml_simple::toml_load(path)?;

    let mut cfg = UserConfig {
        default_account: map.get("default.account").cloned(),
        default_debug: map.get("default.debug").cloned(),
        top_level_repo: None,
        accounts: HashMap::new(),
    };

    let mut top_level = AccountConfig::default();
    let mut top_level_seen = false;

    // Walk all keys; route `repo.*` to top-level, `account.<n>.…`
    // to per-account.
    for (full_key, value) in &map {
        if let Some(suffix) = full_key.strip_prefix("repo.") {
            top_level_seen = true;
            apply_repo_subkey(&mut top_level, suffix, value);
            continue;
        }
        let Some(rest) = full_key.strip_prefix("account.") else {
            continue;
        };
        let Some((name, suffix)) = rest.split_once('.') else {
            continue;
        };
        let Some(repo_suffix) = suffix.strip_prefix("repo.") else {
            continue; // unknown sub-section under [account.<n>]

1	//! `push` subcommand — collapse the dual-repo commit+push+finalize
2	//! ceremony into a single resumable command.
3	//!
4	//! See `notes/chores-05.md > Add push subcommand (0.37.0)` for the
5	//! full design.
6	//!
7	//! Dev-step ladder (expanded from original 4 to 6 after adding an
8	//! integration-test step ahead of the first dogfood):
9	//!
10	//! - `0.37.0-0` — scaffolding: flag surface, `Stage` enum, stub `push()`
11	//! - `0.37.0-1` — state file + stage-dispatch loop with stage stubs;
12	//!   `--status`, `--restart`, `--from`
13	//! - `0.37.0-2` — real stage bodies (commits, bookmarks, push,
14	//!   finalize) + `jj op` snapshot rollback
15	//! - `0.37.0-3` — integration tests + workspace-root refactor
16	//!   (thread `root: &Path` through every stage so fixtures can
17	//!   point them at tempdirs); first `vc-x1 push` dogfood ships
18	//!   this commit
19	//! - `0.37.0-4` — interactivity: two approval gates, `$EDITOR`,
20	//!   message persistence across resumes
21	//! - `0.37.0-5` — polish: `--dry-run`, `--step`, non-tty handling,
22	//!   `.gitignore` coherence warning
23	//! - `0.37.0` — docs + workflow migration (done marker)
24

26	use std::io::IsTerminal;
27	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

37	/// Named stages of the `push` state machine.
38	///
39	/// Used by `--from <stage>` to resume at a specific point and by
40	/// `--status` to report the current position. Ordered top-down so
41	/// `Stage as u8` comparisons reflect progress through the flow.
42	#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, ValueEnum)]
43	#[value(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
44	pub enum Stage {
45	    /// Run fmt / clippy / test / install / retest.
46	    Preflight,
47	    /// Present diff for the first approval gate.
48	    Review,
49	    /// Compose / edit the commit message; present for second gate.
50	    Message,
51	    /// Commit the app repo.
52	    CommitApp,
53	    /// Commit the `.claude` session repo (skipped if empty).
54	    CommitClaude,
55	    /// Advance both bookmarks to `@-`.

  "new_string": "    /// Auto-approve interactive prompts (review gate, message-edit\n    /// confirmation). Required when `--title` / `--body` aren't both\n    /// supplied in non-interactive contexts.\n    #[arg(short = 'y', long)]\n    pub yes: bool,\n}\n\n/// Inputs to the push op, flat, owned, clap-free.\n///\n/// Mirrors `PushArgs`, with the two clap bookmark spellings\n/// (positional `BOOKMARK` and `--bookmark`) collapsed into one\n/// `bookmark` field. The rest map straight over: `--restart`,\n/// `--from` (a `Stage`, a domain type — keeping it), `--step`,\n/// `--status`, `--recheck`, `--no-finalize`, `--dry-run`,\n/// `--title`, `--body`, `--yes` (`yes`).\npub struct PushParams {\n    pub bookmark: Option<String>,\n    pub restart: bool,\n    pub from: Option<Stage>,\n    pub step: bool,\n    pub status: bool,\n    pub recheck: bool,\n    pub no_finalize: bool,\n    pub dry_run: bool,\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n    pub body: Option<String>,\n    pub yes: bool,\n}\n\nimpl From<&PushArgs> for PushParams {\n    /// Convert clap-derived `PushArgs` into the flat `PushParams`,\n    /// collapsing `bookmark_pos` / `bookmark` (clap already enforces\n    /// they don't both appear) into the single `bookmark` field.\n    fn from(a: &PushArgs) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            bookmark: a.bookmark_pos.clone().or_else(|| a.bookmark.clone()),\n            restart: a.restart,\n            from: a.from,\n            step: a.step,\n            status: a.status,\n            recheck: a.recheck,\n            no_finalize: a.no_finalize,\n            dry_run: a.dry_run,\n            title: a.title.clone(),\n            body: a.body.clone(),\n            yes: a.yes,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Current state-file format version — bump when the flat key set"
}
