567	/// Entry point for the `push` subcommand.
568	///
569	/// 0.37.0-5 behavior: interactive flow with two approval gates
570	/// (review + message) plus polish flags: `--dry-run` prints what
571	/// would run without side effects, `--step` pauses between every
572	/// stage, non-tty stdin fails fast when prompts are required,
573	/// `--yes` opts out of all prompts. On any failure in stages 4-6
574	/// (the local mutation window between `commit-app` and
575	/// `bookmark-both`), both repos roll back to the `jj op` snapshot
576	/// recorded at the start of `commit-app`. After `push-app` the
577	/// remote boundary is crossed and recovery is forward-only.
578	pub fn push(args: &PushArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
579	    let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
580	    push_in(&cwd, args)
581	}
582	
583	/// `push` parameterized on the workspace root. CLI dispatch calls
584	/// this with `std::env::current_dir()`; integration tests call it
585	/// with a fixture tempdir so the stage bodies mutate the fixture's
586	/// repos instead of the developer's working tree.
587	pub(crate) fn push_in(
588	    workspace_root: &Path,
589	    args: &PushArgs,
590	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
591	    let layout = resolve_state_layout(workspace_root);
592	
593	    if args.status {
594	        return cmd_status(&layout);
595	    }
596	
597	    if args.dry_run {
598	        info!("push: DRY-RUN — no side effects (no commits, no pushes, no state written)");
599	    }
600	
601	    // Gitignore coherence check (fatal). Only checked when we
602	    // actually have a file path with a parent (resolve_state_layout
603	    // always gives one, so this is effectively always-on).
604	    if let Some(dir_name) = layout
605	        .path
606	        .parent()
607	        .and_then(|p| p.file_name())
608	        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
609	    {
610	        check_gitignore_coherence(workspace_root, dir_name)?;
611	    }
612	
613	    if args.restart && layout.path.exists() {
614	        fs::remove_file(&layout.path)?;
615	        debug!("push: --restart cleared state at {}", layout.path.display());
616	    }
617	
618	    // Load existing state, or require a bookmark (positional or flag)
619	    // to bootstrap one.
620	    let mut state = match PushState::load(&layout.path)? {
621	        Some(s) => s,
622	        None => {
623	            let bookmark = args
624	                .bookmark_pos
625	                .as_deref()
626	                .or(args.bookmark.as_deref())
627	                .ok_or(
628	                    "push: no saved state; a bookmark is required to start a new run \
629	                     — pass it as a positional (`vc-x1 push main`) or via `--bookmark main`",
630	                )?;
631	            PushState::new_for(bookmark)
632	        }
633	    };
634	
635	    // `--from` overrides the resumed stage (does not affect bookmark
636	    // or other persisted fields).
637	    if let Some(from) = args.from {
638	        state.stage = from;
639	    }
640	
641	    run_from(workspace_root, &mut state, args, &layout)
642	}
643	
644	/// Full path of the `.claude` session repo for a given workspace
645	/// root. Centralized so a future layout change (e.g. configurable
646	/// session-repo name) has one caller to update.
647	fn claude_path(workspace_root: &Path) -> PathBuf {
648	    workspace_root.join(".claude")
649	}
```

//! `push` subcommand — collapse the dual-repo commit+push+finalize
//! ceremony into a single resumable command.
//!
//! See `notes/chores-05.md > Add push subcommand (0.37.0)` for the
//! full design.
//!
//! Dev-step ladder (expanded from original 4 to 6 after adding an
//! integration-test step ahead of the first dogfood):
//!
//! - `0.37.0-0` — scaffolding: flag surface, `Stage` enum, stub `push()`
//! - `0.37.0-1` — state file + stage-dispatch loop with stage stubs;
//!   `--status`, `--restart`, `--from`
//! - `0.37.0-2` — real stage bodies (commits, bookmarks, push,
//!   finalize) + `jj op` snapshot rollback
//! - `0.37.0-3` — integration tests + workspace-root refactor
//!   (thread `root: &Path` through every stage so fixtures can
//!   point them at tempdirs); first `vc-x1 push` dogfood ships
//!   this commit
//! - `0.37.0-4` — interactivity: two approval gates, `$EDITOR`,
//!   message persistence across resumes
//! - `0.37.0-5` — polish: `--dry-run`, `--step`, non-tty handling,
//!   `.gitignore` coherence warning
//! - `0.37.0` — docs + workflow migration (done marker)

use std::fs;
use std::io::IsTerminal;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use chrono::Utc;
use clap::{Args, ValueEnum};
use log::{debug, info, warn};

use crate::common::{prompt, run};
use crate::sync::{current_op_id, op_restore};
use crate::toml_simple::toml_load;

/// Named stages of the `push` state machine.
///
/// Used by `--from <stage>` to resume at a specific point and by
/// `--status` to report the current position. Ordered top-down so

{

  "old_string": "    /// Auto-approve interactive prompts (review gate, message-edit\n    /// confirmation). Required when `--title` / `--body` aren't both\n    /// supplied in non-interactive contexts.\n    #[arg(short = 'y', long)]\n    pub yes: bool,\n}\n\n/// Current state-file format version — bump when the flat key set",
  "new_string": "    /// Auto-approve interactive prompts (review gate, message-edit\n    /// confirmation). Required when `--title` / `--body` aren't both\n    /// supplied in non-interactive contexts.\n    #[arg(short = 'y', long)]\n    pub yes: bool,\n}\n\n/// Inputs to the push op, flat, owned, clap-free.\n///\n/// Mirrors `PushArgs`, with the two clap bookmark spellings\n/// (positional `BOOKMARK` and `--bookmark`) collapsed into one\n/// `bookmark` field. The rest map straight over: `restart`, `from`\n/// (a `Stage`, a domain type — kept), `step`, `status`, `recheck`,\n/// `no_finalize`, `dry_run`, `title`, `body`, `yes`.\npub struct PushParams {\n    pub bookmark: Option<String>,\n    pub restart: bool,\n    pub from: Option<Stage>,\n    pub step: bool,\n    pub status: bool,\n    pub recheck: bool,\n    pub no_finalize: bool,\n    pub dry_run: bool,\n    pub title: Option<String>,\n    pub body: Option<String>,\n    pub yes: bool,\n}\n\nimpl From<&PushArgs> for PushParams {\n    /// Convert clap-derived `PushArgs` into the flat `PushParams`,\n    /// collapsing `bookmark_pos` / `bookmark` (clap already enforces\n    /// they don't both appear) into the single `bookmark` field.\n    fn from(a: &PushArgs) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            bookmark: a.bookmark_pos.clone().or_else(|| a.bookmark.clone()),\n            restart: a.restart,\n            from: a.from,\n            step: a.step,\n            status: a.status,\n            recheck: a.recheck,\n            no_finalize: a.no_finalize,\n            dry_run: a.dry_run,\n            title: a.title.clone(),\n            body: a.body.clone(),\n            yes: a.yes,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Current state-file format version — bump when the flat key set"
}

565	
566	/// Entry point for the `push` subcommand.
567	///
568	/// 0.37.0-5 behavior: interactive flow with two approval gates
569	/// (review + message) plus polish flags: `--dry-run` prints what
570	/// would run without side effects, `--step` pauses between every
571	/// stage, non-tty stdin fails fast when prompts are required,
572	/// `--yes` opts out of all prompts. On any failure in stages 4-6
573	/// (the local mutation window between `commit-app` and
574	/// `bookmark-both`), both repos roll back to the `jj op` snapshot
575	/// recorded at the start of `commit-app`. After `push-app` the
576	/// remote boundary is crossed and recovery is forward-only.
577	pub fn push(params: &PushParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
578	    let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
579	    push_in(&cwd, params)
580	}
581	
582	/// `push` parameterized on the workspace root. CLI dispatch calls
583	/// this with `std::env::current_dir()`; integration tests call it
584	/// with a fixture tempdir so the stage bodies mutate the fixture's
585	/// repos instead of the developer's working tree.
586	pub(crate) fn push_in(
587	    workspace_root: &Path,
588	    params: &PushParams,
589	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
590	    let layout = resolve_state_layout(workspace_root);
591	
592	    if params.status {
593	        return cmd_status(&layout);
594	    }
595	
596	    if params.dry_run {
597	        info!("push: DRY-RUN — no side effects (no commits, no pushes, no state written)");
598	    }
599	
600	    // Gitignore coherence check (fatal). Only checked when we
601	    // actually have a file path with a parent (resolve_state_layout
602	    // always gives one, so this is effectively always-on).
603	    if let Some(dir_name) = layout
604	        .path
605	        .parent()
606	        .and_then(|p| p.file_name())
607	        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
608	    {
609	        check_gitignore_coherence(workspace_root, dir_name)?;
610	    }
611	
612	    if params.restart && layout.path.exists() {
613	        fs::remove_file(&layout.path)?;
614	        debug!("push: --restart cleared state at {}", layout.path.display());
615	    }
616	
617	    // Load existing state, or require a bookmark (positional or flag)
618	    // to bootstrap one.
619	    let mut state = match PushState::load(&layout.path)? {
620	        Some(s) => s,
621	        None => {
622	            let bookmark = params
623	                .bookmark_pos
624	                .as_deref()
625	                .or(params.bookmark.as_deref())
626	                .ok_or(
627	                    "push: no saved state; a bookmark is required to start a new run \
628	                     — pass it as a positional (`vc-x1 push main`) or via `--bookmark main`",
629	                )?;
630	            PushState::new_for(bookmark)
631	        }
632	    };
633	
634	    // `--from` overrides the resumed stage (does not affect bookmark
635	    // or other persisted fields).
636	    if let Some(from) = params.from {
637	        state.stage = from;
638	    }
639	
640	    run_from(workspace_root, &mut state, params, &layout)
641	}
642	
643	/// Full path of the `.claude` session repo for a given workspace
644	/// root. Centralized so a future layout change (e.g. configurable
```

pub fn push(_ctx: &Context, params: &PushParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;
    push_in(&cwd, params)
}

/// `push` parameterized on the workspace root. CLI dispatch calls
    if params.restart && layout.path.exists() {
        fs::remove_file(&layout.path)?;
        debug!("push: --restart cleared state at {}", layout.path.display());
    }

    // Load existing state, or require a bookmark (positional or flag)

> AGENT

Step 0.48.0-6 (push → Context + PushParams) complete — the last subcommand port before close-out. Done as a single step (uniform plumbing; no `-6.N` decomposition needed).

- **`Cargo.toml`** — `0.48.0-5` → `0.48.0-6`.
- **`src/push.rs`** — added `PushParams` (flat, owned, clap-free) + `impl From<&PushArgs>`. The `From` collapses the two clap bookmark spellings (`bookmark_pos` positional + `bookmark` flag, which `conflicts_with` each other) into one `bookmark: Option<String>` via `bookmark_pos.or_else(|| bookmark)`; `from` keeps its `Stage` domain type; `recheck` (parsed by `PushArgs` but not yet wired into the stage machine) is carried with `#[allow(dead_code)]` + comment so the conversion stays total. `push(args)` → `push(_ctx: &Context, params: &PushParams)`; `ctx` stops at `push()` — `push_in` and all the threaded fns (`run_from`, `run_stage`, every `stage_*`, `resolve_message`) take `&PushParams` only (matches how `sync_repos` takes `&SyncParams`). `args` → `params` rename throughout; `push_in`'s bookmark resolve drops the `.or()` dance for `params.bookmark.as_deref()`. Added `use crate::context::Context;` and a doc note on the unused `ctx`.
- **`src/main.rs`** — `Push` dispatch arm builds `Context::load(cli.log)` + `PushParams::from`, same shape as the other arms.
- **`src/push/integration_tests.rs`** — `test_args` → `test_params` (builds `PushParams`; `bookmark_pos`+`bookmark` → `bookmark`); call sites + the `args1` local renamed. `src/push/tests.rs` untouched (clap-parse tests on `PushArgs`).
- **`ARCHITECTURE.md`** — module map row + Migration A status table row for `push` → `done (0.48.0-6)`.
- **`notes/chores-09.md`** — added `## push → Context + PushParams (0.48.0-6)` subsection.
- **`notes/todo.md`** — In-Progress ladder: `0.48.0-6 push` marked `(current)` then `(done)`.

Cargo cycle: `fmt` clean, `clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, `cargo test` 360 + 5 + 4 + 5 all pass, `cargo install --path . --locked` OK, `vc-x1 -V` → `0.48.0-6`, `vc-x1 push --help` / `--status` work. (Note: the `jj diff` for `push.rs` renders `args`→`params` changes inline as `argsparams` — that's jj's word-level diff display, not a literal in the file; verified the file has no stray `argsparams`.)

After this, the remaining ladder item is `0.48.0` close-out (drop suffix, todo→Done, Migration A table all-done).

Work complete. Please review. On approval I'll prep the commit title and body for a second approval before pushing.

230	- **Single-step** (recommended for mechanical/focused changes): bump
231	  directly to `X.Y.Z`, implement in one commit. Simpler history.
232	- **Multi-step** (for exploratory/large changes): bump to `X.Y.Z-0`,
233	  implement across multiple commits incrementing the numeric
234	  suffix. The final commit drops the suffix.
235	
236	The plan should recommend one approach and get user approval before
237	starting.
238	
239	For multi-step:
240	
241	1. Bump version to `X.Y.Z-0` with the plan and commit as a chore
242	   marker.
243	2. Implement in one or more `X.Y.Z-N` commits (increment N as
244	   needed).
245	3. Final commit bumps to `X.Y.Z` (no suffix), updates
246	   `notes/todo.md` and `notes/chores-*.md` — this is the "done"
247	   marker.
248	
249	Multi-step cycles surface the ladder at the top of
250	`notes/todo.md > ## In Progress` as a bullet list with `(done)` /
251	`(current)` markers — see the file's intro paragraph for the
252	format. When starting a new step, the *first* edit is to mark
253	that step `(current)` in `notes/todo.md` — before any code/doc
254	work — so the In Progress view reflects what's actually
255	happening. The flip back to `(done)` is part of the pre-commit
256	checklist (item 6).
257	
258	**Why numeric suffixes (`-0`, `-1`, …) rather than `-devN`:**
259	semver pre-release identifiers may consist of a single numeric
```

=== chores-09 tail ===
  `FixDescParams::from` (same shape as the `ValidateDesc` arm).
- Added missing `//!` module docstring and `///` on
  `fix_desc()` (pre-existing gaps, fixed in passing).

## push → Context + PushParams (0.48.0-6)

Step 6 — last subcommand port before close-out. Biggest module
(`PushArgs` threaded through `push_in` / `run_from` / `run_stage`
/ every `stage_*` / `resolve_message`); uniform plumbing, no
behavior change. One step, not decomposed into `-6.N`.

- `PushParams` + `From<&PushArgs>` in `push.rs`; `from` keeps the
  `Stage` domain type.
- `push(args)` → `push(_ctx: &Context, params: &PushParams)`;
  `ctx` stops at `push()` (`push_in` + stage fns take
  `&PushParams` only, like `sync_repos` / `&SyncParams`). `args`
  → `params` through the threaded fns.
- `main.rs` `Push` arm: `Context::load(cli.log)` +
  `PushParams::from`.
- `push/integration_tests.rs`: `test_args` → `test_params`;
  `push/tests.rs` untouched (clap-parse tests on `PushArgs`).
- Two `PushArgs` quirks the port had to absorb —
  [dual bookmark parameters](#push-dual-bookmark-parameters) and
  the [unimplemented `recheck` flag](#push-unimplemented-recheck-flag)
  — both also queued in `notes/todo.md`.

### push: dual bookmark parameters

`PushArgs` carries two fields for one logical value —
`bookmark_pos` (positional `BOOKMARK`) and `bookmark`
(`--bookmark` flag), mutually `conflicts_with`. `From<&PushArgs>`
collapses them: `bookmark_pos.clone().or_else(|| bookmark.clone())`.
That `or_else` is a smell forced by the CLI shape (a positional
*and* a flag for the same thing). Better: a single positional
with `--bookmark` as a true clap alias, or drop one spelling.
Queued as a todo.

### push: unimplemented `recheck` flag

`PushArgs.recheck` (`--recheck`, "re-run preflight on resume") is
parsed but never read — the stage machine has no
skip-preflight-on-resume path. Mirrored into `PushParams` with
`#[allow(dead_code)]` to keep the conversion total, but a dead
field plus an `allow` is debt. Implement the behavior or remove
the flag. Queued as a todo.

169	   `sync -h` is two-column but `init -h` is over-under —
170	   visual inconsistency.
171	1. Replace "Step N" log prefixes with single-word
172	   `label: body` convention (`bookmark`, `provision`,
173	   `colocate`, `cross-ref`, `symlink`, …); indent labels
174	   under per-side `code:` / `bot:` headers in dual.
175	   Originally planned as 0.41.1-6.7; deferred.
176	1. Consider renaming the `.vc-config.toml` `[workspace]`
177	   section. Rust readers expect `[workspace]` to mean a
178	   Cargo workspace, which a vc-x1 dual-repo isn't.
179	   Candidates: `[repo-list]`, `[project]`, `[dual-repo]`.
180	   Breaking change — needs migration story (read both
181	   names during a transition cycle, or one-shot rewrite
182	   in `vc-x1 sync`/`init` on first contact). Drives the
183	   broader "stop saying workspace in user-facing surfaces"
184	   sweep.
185	1. Add `status` (alias `st`) subcommand: `jj st` across both
186	   repos in one shot. Uses `--scope` from day one. This is
187	   natural home for the working-copy signal called out and
188	   it needs to include remotes, like remotes/origin/main. [54].
189	1. `vc-x1 init --dry-run` should bypass the
190	   `--repo-remote` path-existence preflight (currently fires
191	   before the dry-run early-return; observed dogfooding
192	   2026-04-24).
193	1. vc-x1 push: `--squash` flag. Squashes WC into `@-` via
194	   `--ignore-immutable` and force-pushes; needs
195	   `--force-with-lease`-equivalent + state-sanity preflight in
196	   place first. [57]
197	1. vc-x1 push: `--message-file PATH` flag. Git-style commit
198	   message file (first line = title, blank, rest = body).
199	   Alternative to `--title` + `--body`. [58]
200	1. Mirror `--check` / `--no-check` onto `vc-x1 push` (forwards
201	   through to the preflight `vc-x1 sync` invocation).
202	   0.37.1 hard-codes `--check`; default stays `--check`.
203	1. Add `validate-repo` subcommand: diagnostic that runs all
204	   `verify_*` checks (tracking, push state freshness, ochid
205	   integrity, conflicts, config sanity, working-copy state)
206	   and reports per-check pass/fail. Exit code = number of
207	   failed checks. Implementation: promote
208	   `verify_state_sanity` / `verify_completion_sanity` from
209	   push.rs to `common.rs`. [69]
210	1. sync: surface working-copy state in the up-to-date summary
211	   (per-repo pending-files count or compact stat). Wording-only
212	   fix shipped in 0.37.1; this is the design+impl. [54]
213	1. bm-track silent-when-clean refinement. Print on entry/exit
214	   only when state isn't fully tracked or when exit state
215	   differs from entry. [62]
216	1. "Oh shit" revert — post-success undo via `.vc-x1-ops/`
217	   anchor dir. Idea-stage; every repo-mutating command drops a
218	   pre-op snapshot, `vc-x1 undo` restores both repos. [57]
219	1. Restructure templates: replace separate `vc-template-x1` +
220	   `vc-template-x1.claude` repos with a single `vc-template-x1`
221	   that has `.claude/` as a subdir (covers `LICENSE-*` etc. for
222	   both sides in one place). Updates to `vc-x1 init` / `clone`
223	   needed for the new layout.
224	1. Source-code design ref sweep + CLAUDE.md codification:
225	   adopt section-name + `blob/main/...` URL pattern for source
226	   code refs to designs; codify in CLAUDE.md alongside the
227	   existing markdown ref conventions. Sweep targets:
228	   src/push.rs lines 4, 121, 645, 1219. [68]
229	1. Richer bookmark enumeration: per-bookmark remote presence + tracking status [52]
230	1. Per-line/per-thread runtime log points (future, maybe) [36]
231	1. Add Windows symlink support via `std::os::windows::fs::symlink_dir` [37]
232	1. Add "::" revision syntax for jj compatibility
233	1. Add -p, --parents, -c, --children so parent and child counts can be asymmetric
234	1. Add integration tests in tests/ for subcommands using temp jj repos (tempfile crate)
235	1. Fix .claude repo history: dev0 through dev2 sessions squashed into wrong commit [4],[5]
236	1. Add `vc-x1 setup` subcommand: completions install, .claude repo init, symlink setup [27]
237	1. Add dynamic revision completion via `ArgValueCompleter` (jj doesn't complete revsets either) [28],[29]
238	1. Test-tempdir override resolution chain. Both
239	   `src/test_helpers::unique_base` and
240	   `tests/common/unique_base` currently use
241	   `std::env::temp_dir()` (= `$TMPDIR`). Generalize to
242	   resolve in priority order: explicit env var (e.g.
243	   `VC_X1_TEST_TMPDIR`) → user config
244	   (`~/.config/vc-x1/config.toml`) → local
245	   `.vc-config.toml` → `std::env::temp_dir()` fallback.
246	   Useful when a developer wants tests on a tmpfs / SSD /
247	   project-local path without exporting `TMPDIR` globally.
248	   Open question: do we also expose a CLI parameter
249	   (e.g. `vc-x1 --workspace-tmp …`)? Test binaries can't
250	   easily accept arbitrary flags via `cargo test --`, so
251	   env is the realistic surface for tests; for the
252	   `vc-x1` binary itself a flag is feasible but unclear
253	   it adds value over the resolution chain.
254

185	## Migration A — args → Context + Params
186	
187	Port each subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to
188	`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)`, adding an
189	`XxxParams` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the
190	conversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0)
191	is the worked example; `finalize` (0.46.0) added the `TryFrom`
192	/ `Context.log` variant; the `0.48.0-N` cycle sweeps the rest.
193	Per-port status is the table below; the live checklist is the
194	"Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling" item in
195	[`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md).
196	
197	| Subcommand | Status |
198	| --- | --- |
199	| `init` | done (0.44.0) — `init(&Context, &InitParams)`; `From<&InitArgs>` |
200	| `finalize` | done (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`; `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` (fallible boundary); `--log` moved onto `Context` |
201	| `sync` | done (0.48.0-3) — `sync(&Context, &SyncParams)`; `From<&SyncArgs>` |
202	| `chid` | not started |
203	| `desc` | not started |
204	| `list` | not started |
205	| `show` | not started |
206	| `validate-desc` | done (0.48.0-4) — `validate_desc(&Context, &ValidateDescParams)`; `From<&ValidateDescArgs>` |
207	| `fix-desc` | done (0.48.0-5) — `fix_desc(&Context, &FixDescParams)`; `From<&FixDescArgs>` |
208	| `clone` | done (0.48.0-2) — `clone_repo(&Context, &CloneParams)`; `From<&CloneArgs>` |
209	| `push` | done (0.48.0-6) — `push(&Context, &PushParams)`; `From<&PushArgs>` (collapses the two bookmark spellings) |
210	| `symlink` | done (0.48.0-1) — `symlink(&Context, &SymlinkParams)`; `From<&SymlinkArgs>` |
211	
212	The rest are planned as one multi-step cycle (`0.48.0-N`), one
213	step per subcommand (`chid`/`desc`/`list`/`show` ride with the
214	separate "CommonArgs sweep" since their Migration A and B are
215	entangled).
216	
217	Out of scope for the early ports (deferred until a real
218	consumer surfaces): typed errors, returned outcomes vs
219	`println!`, the `ProgressSink`, and `Context` fields beyond
220	`UserConfig` + the `--log` path (`finalize` surfaced `log`).
221	
222	## Migration B — per-subcommand flags → `src/options_flags/`
223	
224	Independently of Migration A, per-subcommand inline `#[arg]`
```

  "old_string": "## Migration A — args → Context + Params\n\nPort each subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to\n`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)`, adding an\n`XxxParams` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the\nconversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0)\nis the worked example; `finalize` (0.46.0) added the `TryFrom`\n/ `Context.log` variant; the `0.48.0-N` cycle sweeps the rest.\nPer-port status is the table below; the live checklist is the\n\"Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling\" item in\n[`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md).\n\n| Subcommand | Status |\n| --- | --- |\n| `init` | done (0.44.0) — `init(&Context, &InitParams)`; `From<&InitArgs>` |\n| `finalize` | done (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`; `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` (fallible boundary); `--log` moved onto `Context` |\n| `sync` | done (0.48.0-3) — `sync(&Context, &SyncParams)`; `From<&SyncArgs>` |\n| `chid` | not started |\n| `desc` | not started |\n| `list` | not started |\n| `show` | not started |\n| `validate-desc` | done (0.48.0-4) — `validate_desc(&Context, &ValidateDescParams)`; `From<&ValidateDescArgs>` |\n| `fix-desc` | done (0.48.0-5) — `fix_desc(&Context, &FixDescParams)`; `From<&FixDescArgs>` |\n| `clone` | done (0.48.0-2) — `clone_repo(&Context, &CloneParams)`; `From<&CloneArgs>` |\n| `push` | done (0.48.0-6) — `push(&Context, &PushParams)`; `From<&PushArgs>` (collapses the two bookmark spellings) |\n| `symlink` | done (0.48.0-1) — `symlink(&Context, &SymlinkParams)`; `From<&SymlinkArgs>` |\n\nThe rest are planned as one multi-step cycle (`0.48.0-N`), one\nstep per subcommand (`chid`/`desc`/`list`/`show` ride with the\nseparate \"CommonArgs sweep\" since their Migration A and B are\nentangled).\n\nOut of scope for the early ports (deferred until a real\nconsumer surfaces): typed errors, returned outcomes vs\n`println!`, the `ProgressSink`, and `Context` fields beyond\n`UserConfig` + the `--log` path (`finalize` surfaced `log`).",
  "new_string": "## Migration A — args → Context + Params\n\nPort each subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to\n`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)`, adding an\n`XxxParams` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the\nconversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0) is\nthe worked example; `finalize` (0.46.0) added the `TryFrom` /\n`Context.log` variant; the `0.48.0` cycle swept the rest of the\nstandalone subcommands. Only `chid` / `desc` / `list` / `show`\nremain — their Migration A is bundled into the future\n\"CommonArgs sweep\" (Migration A + B are entangled for the\n`CommonArgs`-flattening subcommands). The live checklist is the\n\"Subcommand layer / CLI decoupling\" item in\n[`notes/todo.md`](notes/todo.md).\n\n| Subcommand | Status |\n| --- | --- |\n| `init` | done (0.44.0) — `init(&Context, &InitParams)`; `From<&InitArgs>` |\n| `finalize` | done (0.46.0) — `finalize(&Context, &FinalizeParams)`; `TryFrom<&FinalizeArgs>` (fallible boundary); `--log` moved onto `Context` |\n| `symlink` | done (0.48.0-1) — `symlink(&Context, &SymlinkParams)`; `From<&SymlinkArgs>` |\n| `clone` | done (0.48.0-2) — `clone_repo(&Context, &CloneParams)`; `From<&CloneArgs>` |\n| `sync` | done (0.48.0-3) — `sync(&Context, &SyncParams)`; `From<&SyncArgs>` |\n| `validate-desc` | done (0.48.0-4) — `validate_desc(&Context, &ValidateDescParams)`; `From<&ValidateDescArgs>` |\n| `fix-desc` | done (0.48.0-5) — `fix_desc(&Context, &FixDescParams)`; `From<&FixDescArgs>` |\n| `push` | done (0.48.0-6) — `push(&Context, &PushParams)`; `From<&PushArgs>` (collapses the two bookmark spellings) |\n| `chid` | deferred — rides the CommonArgs sweep (Migration A + B entangled) |\n| `desc` | deferred — rides the CommonArgs sweep (Migration A + B entangled) |\n| `list` | deferred — rides the CommonArgs sweep (Migration A + B entangled) |\n| `show` | deferred — rides the CommonArgs sweep (Migration A + B entangled) |\n\nOut of scope for these ports (deferred until a real consumer\nsurfaces): typed errors, returned outcomes vs `println!`, the\n`ProgressSink`, and `Context` fields beyond `UserConfig` + the\n`--log` path (`finalize` surfaced `log`)."
